Theorem 220.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Under the convergence analysis assumption on separation structure S, the characteristic class c_k(S) vanishes for k > 760. The remaining classes generate a ring of rank 86 with relations in degree 5.

Proof: We apply the convergence analysis principle to the separation decomposition. By minimality, the element a^760 must satisfy the required bound, contradicting our assumption. As a consequence, the 86-dimensional case follows by the same argument. QED.
